# 1999 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** AIM door · Napster theater · Y2K
**Star:** AIM sign-on · `itt99-aim` · `sites/aim/index.html`
**ILS June:** 3,177,453 · 280,866,670
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 422
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt99-aim`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/1999/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/1999/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 1999 — peak bubble · Y2K
2. AIM sign-on — screen name REAL
3. Napster — search a track
4. Google — funded, still empty
5. Blogger · Y2K clock · SourceForge
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt99-aim`

**Room:** `years/1999/sites/aim/index.html`
**Hook:** `data-aim-signon`
**Nostalgia:** AIM sign-on — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Empty screen name · Napster-as-gold trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt99-aim`.

**Complete writes**
- Screen name ≥2 · Sign On. Writes itt99-aim.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `1999` · key `itt99-aim`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-aim` — do not write `itt98-*` or `itt00-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Napster → `years/1999/sites/napster/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 422 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt99-ab-lx` · sites/about/index.html

**Open:** `/years/1999/sites/about/index.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="ab-lx"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “about leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `google` · `amazon`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="ab-lx"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt99-ab-lx']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt99-aim` stays empty. `itt98-ab-lx` and `itt00-ab-lx` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “about leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="ab-lx"]`.
- `localStorage['itt99-ab-lx']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1999", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt99-ab-lx`.
- Does **not** write `itt99-aim`.
- **Next:** Google leftover → `/years/1999/sites/google/index.html`
